A program started with `&` or `nohup`\nis still in that group, and in a form like `cd /app && nohup python3 autopilot.py > log 2>&1 &`\nthe shell that waits for it still holds the tool's output. Standard\nlibrary only; needs a POSIX system.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport argparse\nimport os\nimport subprocess\nimport sys\nfrom typing import Optional, Sequence\n\n\ndef start(command: Sequence[str], log: str = \"autopilot.log\") -> int:\n    \"\"\"Start `command` detached, appending its output to `log`; return its process id.\"\"\"\n    environment = dict(os.environ, PYTHONUNBUFFERED=\"1\")\n    with open(log, \"ab\") as stream:\n        process = subprocess.Popen(list(command), stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL, stdout=stream,\n                                   stderr=subprocess.STDOUT, start_new_session=True,\n                                   close_fds=True, env=environment)\n    return process.pid\n\n\ndef main(argv: Optional[Sequence[str]] = None) -> int:\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(\n        description=\"Start a program that keeps running after this command returns.\",\n        usage=\"python3 detach.py [--log FILE] command [argument ...]\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--log\", default=\"autopilot.log\", help=\"file the program's output is appended to\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"command\", nargs=argparse.REMAINDER, help=\"the program and its arguments\")\n    args = parser.parse_args(argv)\n    command = args.command[1:] if args.command[:1] == [\"--\"] else args.command\n    if not command:\n        parser.error(\"give the program to start, for example: python3 autopilot.py\")\n    try:\n        pid = start(command, args.log)\n    except OSError as failure:\n        print(\"could not start {}: {}\".format(command[0], failure), file=sys.stderr)\n        return 1\n    print(\"started pid {}; its output goes to {}; stop it with: kill {}\".format(\n        pid, os.path.abspath(args.log), pid))\n    return 0\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    sys.exit(main())","lineStart":1,"lineEnd":61,"totalLines":61,"truncated":false}},"title":"app/detach.py","time":{"start":1794709623874,"end":1794709623884}},"metadata":{"openai":{"itemId":"fc_0af87fd5359f0961016af91877cb3887d1b43878f0d9a0567c"}},"id":"prt_1dd079440001e93jkK23yXKtzN","sessionID":"ses_e22f8c3a2ffeJ5y6VszjITVRnJ","messageID":"msg_1dd0769fc001G6R1g4Asf0Pvb5"}}

        for dx, dy in [(0, 0), (1.25, 0), (-1.25, 0), (0, 1.25), (0, -1.25)])\n+            return safe[key]\n+        rows, cols, costs, physical = [], [], [], []\n+        slipbase = {k: sum(v)/2 = {}\n+        log('GRAPH', n, 'nodes', len(costs), 'edges')\n+\n+    def nearest(self, p):\n+        a = np.asarray(p)\n+        ix = self.goodids[np.argmin(np.sum((self.xy[self.goodids]-a)**2, is_safe(x, y):\n+            key = (x, y)\n+            if key not in safe:\n+                safe[key] = all(self.s.inside(x + dx, y + dy) and\n+                                self.s.slope(x + dx, y + dy) < .315\n+                                for dx, dy in [(0, 0), (1.25, 0), (-1.25, 0), (0, 1.25), (0, -1.25)])\n+            return safe[key]\n+        rows, cols, costs, physical = [], [], [], []\n+        slipbase = {k: sum(v)/2 for k, v in self.physics['slip_base_ranges'].items()}\n+        self.good = np.array([is_safe(*p) for p in self.xy])\n+        for a, (x, y) in enumerate(self.xy):\n+            if not self.good[a]:\n+                continue\n+            i, j = a % self.nx, a // self.nx\n+            for di, dj in [(1, 0), (0, 1), (1, 1), (-1, 1)]:\n+                ni, nj = i+di, j+dj\n+                if not (0 <= ni < self.nx and 0 <= nj < self.ny):\n+                    continue\n+                b = nj * self.nx + ni\n+                if not self.good[b] or not is_safe(x+2.5*di, y+2.5*dj):\n+                    continue\n+                length = 5*math.hypot(di, dj)\n+                ux, uy = di/math.hypot(di,dj), dj/math.hypot(di,dj)\n+                for src, dst, sign in [(a,b,1), (b,a,-1)]:\n+                    ticks = energy = 0.\n+                    for frac in [.2,.5,.8]:\n+                        _, gx, gy, code = self.s.sample(x+5*di*frac, y+5*dj*frac)\n+                        name = TERRAIN_CODES[code]\n+                        grade = sign*(gx*ux+gy*uy)\n+                        slip = min(.94, slipbase[name]+self.physics['slip_grade_gain'][name]*max(grade,0))\n+                        wheel = length/3/(1-slip)\n+                        ticks += wheel/5\n+                        energy += wheel*self.physics['drive_cost_per_wheel_m'][name]+2*length/3*max(0,grade)\n+                    # Slight energy/risk preference, preserving predominantly time-shortest paths.\n+                    weight = ticks + .07*max(0, energy-1.9*ticks)\n+                    rows.append(src); cols.append(dst); costs.append(weight); physical.append(ticks)\n+        n = len(self.xy)\n+        self.graph = csr_matrix((costs,(rows,cols)), shape=(n,n))\n+        self.reverse = self.graph.transpose().tocsr()\n+        self.goodids = np.flatnonzero(self.good)\n+        self.fields = {}\n+        self.cache = {}\n+        log('GRAPH', n, 'nodes', len(costs), 'edges')\n+\n+    def nearest(self, p):\n+        a = np.asarray(p)\n+        ix = self.goodids[np.argmin(np.sum((self.xy[self.goodids]-a)**2, axis=1))]\n+        return int(ix)\n+\n+    def field(self, p):\n+        key = tuple(p)\n+        if key not in self.fields:\n+            node = self.nearest(p)\n+            ds, pred = dijkstra(self.reverse, indices=node, return_predecessors=True)\n+            self.fields[key] = (ds, pred, node)\n+        return self.fields[key]\n+\n+    def time_between(self, a, b):\n+        key = (tuple(a),tuple(b))\n+        if key not in self.cache:\n+            ds, _, end = self.field(b)\n+            start = self.nearest(a)\n+            self.cache[key] = float(ds[start]) + (math.dist(a,self.xy[start])+math.dist(b,self.xy[end]))/4.0\n+        return self.cache[key]\n+\n+    def clear(self, a, b, margin=.8):\n+        length = math.dist(a,b)\n+        for f in np.linspace(0,1,max(2,int(length/.8)+1)):\n+            x,y = np.array(a)*(1-f)+np.array(b)*f\n+            for dx,dy in [(0,0),(margin,0),(-margin,0),(0,margin),(0,-margin)]:\n+                if not self.s.inside(x+dx,y+dy) or self.s.slope(x+dx,y+dy)>.325:\n+                    return False\n+        return True\n+\n+    def waypoint(self, p, goal):\n+        if math.dist(p,goal)<18 and self.clear(p,goal):\n+            return np.array(goal)\n+        ds, pred, end = self.field(goal)\n+        # Attach to a nearby visible node, including progress toward the goal.\n+        i,j = round(p[0]/5),round(p[1]/5)\n+        candidates=[]\n+        for dj in range(-2,3):\n+            for di in range(-2,3):\n+                ii,jj=i+di,j+dj\n+                if 0<=ii<self.nx and 0<=jj<self.ny:\n+                    a=jj*self.nx+ii\n+                    if math.isfinite(ds[a]):\n+                        candidates.append((ds[a]+math.dist(p,self.xy[a])/4.,a))\n+        candidates.sort()\n+        node=None\n+        for _,a in candidates:\n+            if self.clear(p,self.xy[a],.6):\n+                node=a;break\n+        if node is None:\n+            node=self.nearest(p)\n+        path=[self.xy[node]]\n+        for _ in range(8):\n+            if node==end: break\n+            node=int(pred[node])\n+            if node<0: break\n+            path.append(self.xy[node])\n+        result=path[0]\n+        for a in path:\n+            if math.dist(p,a)>17: break\n+            if self.clear(p,a): result=a\n+            else: break\n+        return result\n+\n+\n+class Estimator:\n+    def __init__(self, surface, rules, packet):\n+        self.s=surface; self.ph=rules['physics']\n+        self.names=list(self.ph['slip_base_ranges'])\n+        self.x=np.array(list(packet['position'])+[0.]+[sum(self.ph['slip_base_ranges'][k])/2 for k in self.names])\n+        self.P=np.diag([.0001,.0001,math.radians(5)**2/3]+[(self.ph['slip_base_ranges'][k][1]-self.ph['slip_base_ranges'][k][0])**2/12 for k in self.names])\n+        self.tick=0; self.battery=100.; self.eff=.9\n+\n+    def sim(self, x, cmd):\n+        out=x.copy()\n+        if cmd.get('action')!='drive': return out,0.,0.,None\n+        slips={k:float(x[3+i]) for i,k in enumerate(self.names)}\n+        r=drive(self.s,self.ph,float(x[0]),float(x[1]),cmd['heading_deg']+math.degrees(x[2]),cmd['metres'],slips)\n+        out[:2]=r['x'],r['y']\n+        return out,r['ground'],r['energy'],r['stop']\n+\n+    def observe(self, packet, cmd):\n+        old=self.x.copy(); P=self.P.copy()\n+        driving=cmd.get('action')=='drive'\n+        eps=[.08,.08,.002,.003,.003,.003,.003]\n+        def linearize(x):\n+            z,ground,energy,stop=self.sim(x,cmd)\n+            F=np.eye(7); H=np.zeros(7)\n+            for i in range(7):\n+                xx=x.copy();xx[i]+=eps[i]\n+                zz,gg,_,_=self.sim(xx,cmd)\n+                F[:2,i]=(zz[:2]-z[:2])/eps[i]\n+                H[i]=(gg-ground)/eps[i]\n+            F[:2,:]=np.clip(F[:2,:],-15,15)\n+            return z,ground,energy,stop,F,H\n+        if driving:\n+            z,g,en,stop,F,H=linearize(old)\n+            # Visual odometry strongly constrains slip, without using noisy heading telemetry.\n+            if g>1.5 and packet['wheel_odometry']>cmd['metres']-.1 and not stop:\n+                R=max(.035,.05*g)**2+.035**2\n+                K=P@H/(H@P@H+R)\n+                innovation=np.clip(packet['visual_odometry']-g,-1.,1.)\n+                old+=K*innovation\n+                P-=np.outer(K,H@P)\n+                z,g,en,stop,F,H=linearize(old)\n+            self.x=z\n+            Q=np.diag([.0225,.0225,math.radians(.2)**2,1e-7,1e-7,2e-7,1e-7])\n+            # Rougher near cell boundaries; position observations K=self.P@H.T@np.linalg.inv(S)\n+        innovation=np.array(packet['position'])-self.x[:2]\n+        self.x+=K@innovation\n+        self.P=(np.eye(7)-K@H)@self.P\n+        self.P=(self.P+self.P.T)/2\n+        self.x[2]=np.clip(self.x[2],-.20,.20)\n+        for i,k in enumerate(self.names):\n+            low,high=self.ph['slip_base_ranges'][k]\n+            self.x[3+i]=np.clip(self.x[3+i],low,high)\n+        self.tick=packet['tick'];self.battery=packet['battery']\n+        if packet['charge']>.01 and packet['battery']<95:\n+            sun=2.8*math.sin(math.pi*(self.tick+56)/532)\n+            self.eff=.8*self.eff+.2*packet['charge']/sun\n+\n+    def predict(self, until, commands):\n+        x=self.x.copy();b=self.battery\n+        for t in range(self.tick+1,until+1):\n+            cmd=commands.get(t,{'action':'wait'})\n+            x,_,energy,_=self.sim(x,cmd)\n+   targets=[t for t in self.world['targets'] if t['id'] not in excluded]\n+        n=len(targets)\n+        if not n:return []\n+        points=[position]+[[t['x'],t['y']] for t in          prev=a\n+            tm+=mat[prev,n+1]\n+            if not math.isfinite(tm):return -1e6,tm,0\n+            score=value-.014*(tm-tick)-.38*max(0,tm-budget)-.018*max(0,tm-budget)**2\n+            return score,tm,value\n+        best=[];bestscore=evaluate(best)[0]\n+        # Greedy insertion builds geographically coherent clusters.\n+        for power in [.6,1.,1.5]:\n+                        q=(vv-v)/max(1,tt-tm)**power\n+                        if q>quality:quality=q;choice=r\n+                if choice is None:break\n+                route=choice\n+ score=evaluate(r)[0]\n+            if score>bestscore:best,bestscore=r,score\n+        route=best[:];score=bestscore\n+        for it in range(iterations):\n+            if     a=self.rng.choice(avail);r.insert(self.rng.randrange(len(r)+1),a)\n+            elif u<.47 and r:\n+                r.pop(self.rng.randrange(len(r)))\n+            elif u<.72 and len(r)>1:\n+                a=r.pop(self.rng.randrange(len(r)));r.insert(self.rng.randrange(len(r)+1),a)\n+            elif u<.87 and len(r)>1:\n+ uncertainty in the position estimate.\n+        safe = {}\n+        def is_safe(x, y):\n+            key = (x, y)\n+            if key not in safe:\n+                safe[key] = all(self.s.inside(x +    return safe[key]\n+        rows, cols, costs, physical = [], [], [], []\n+        slipbase = {k: sum(v)/2 for k, v in self.physics['slip_base_ranges'].items()}\n+        self.good =           for di, dj in [(1, 0), (0, 1), (1, 1), (-1, 1)]:\n+                ni, nj = i+di, j+dj\n+                if not (0 <= ni < self.nx and 0 <= nj < self.ny):\n+                    continue\n+                b = nj * self.nx + ni\n+                if not self.good[b] or not is_safe(x+2.5*di, y+2.5*dj):\n+                    continue\n+                length = 5*math.hypot(di, dj)\n+                ux, uy = di/math.hypot(di,dj), dj/math.hypot(di,dj)\n+                for src, dst, sign in [(a,b,1), (b,a,-1)]:\n+                    ticks = energy = 0.\n+                    for frac in      ticks += wheel/5\n+                        energy += wheel*self.physics['drive_cost_per_wheel_m'][name]+2*length/3*max(0,grade)\n+                    # Slight energy/risk preference, preserving predominantly time-shortest paths.\n+                    weight = ticks + .07*max(0, energy-1.9*ticks)\n+                    rows.append(src); cols.append(dst); costs.append(weight);    ix = self.goodids[np.argmin(np.sum((self.xy[self.goodids]-a)**2, axis=1))]\n+        return int(ix)\n+\n+    def field(self, p):\n+        key = tuple(p)\n+        if key not in self.fields:\n+            node = self.nearest(p)\n+            ds, pred = dijkstra(self.reverse, indices=node, return_predecessors=True)\n+            self.fields[key] = (ds, pred, node)\n+        return self.nearest(a)\n+            self.cache[key] = float(ds[start]) + (math.dist(a,self.xy[start])+math.dist(b,self.xy[end]))/4.0\n+        return self.cache[key]\n+\n+    def clear(self, a, b, margin=.8):\n+        length = math.dist(a,b)\n+        for f in np.linspace(0,1,max(2,int(length/.8)+1)):\n+            x,y = np.array(a)*(1-f)+np.array(b)*f\n+            for dx,dy in    def waypoint(self, p, goal):\n+        if math.dist(p,goal)<18 and self.clear(p,goal):\n+            return np.array(goal)\n+        ds, pred, end = self.field(goal)\n+        # Attach to a nearby eps=[.08,.08,.002,.003,.003,.003,.003]\n+        def linearize(x):\n+            z,ground,energy,stop=self.sim(x,cmd)\n+            F=np.eye(7); H=np.zeros(7)\n+            for i in range(7):\n+
